02 · COVERAGE MAP

Coverage and evidence levels

Architecture & ownershipverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

持久化入口、流事件状态机、重试回滚、空响应/上下文恢复与 soft interrupt。

Model & contextverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

统一 Provider、MultiProvider 路由、认证/模型切换、provider-native tool/compaction。

Model & contextverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

cache-aware prompt、三种压缩策略、紧急恢复、后台跨会话 memory agent。

Tool registration & executionverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

typed registry、工具快照、batch、输出预算、前后台命令和 stdin。

Security, approval & permissionsverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

allowed/disabled tools、pre_tool hook、危险命令 gate；未发现默认 OS 容器/系统沙箱。

Connectors, plugins & MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

shared pool/per-session client、stdio JSON-RPC、schema late registration、按需连接。

Instructions & extension surfaceverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

AGENTS.md、overlay、preferred-tools、跨工具 skills、lifecycle hooks、自开发模式。

Collaboration & sub-agentsverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

轻 fan-out、持久 Swarm、deep DAG/gates/artifacts、消息/频道、崩溃回收。

Observability & replayverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

snapshot+JSONL、损坏恢复、事件日志/metrics/opt-out telemetry、MIT。

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

Architecture & ownership

4 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 每个用户 turn 先写盘，再进入可恢复的流式循环

SOURCE FACT

run_once/run_once_capture 先把用户消息加入 Session 并 save，再调用 run_turn；远程流入口还先注入跨 Agent 通知、图片和本轮 system reminder，并在 turn 前后触发 observer hooks。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

模型还没开口，用户输入已经落账；即使后面 API 或工具出错，恢复时也不会连问题本身都丢掉。

IMPLICATION

耐崩溃性强，但每轮和工具结果频繁保存会增加本地 I/O，需要 journal 快路径配合。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 4–18
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 19–35
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 119301ac9d60 · open ledger commit ↗
crates/jcode-app-core/src/agent/turn_execution.rs:4–35PINNED ↗
    4  impl Agent {
    5      /// Run a single turn with the given user message
    6      pub async fn run_once(&mut self, user_message: &str) -> Result<()> {
    7          self.add_message(
    8              Role::User,
    9              vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   10                  text: user_message.to_string(),
   11                  cache_control: None,
   12              }],
   13          );
   14          self.session.save()?;
   15          if trace_enabled() {
   16              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", self.session.id);
   17          }
   18          let _ = self.run_turn(true).await?;
   19          Ok(())
   20      }
   21
   22      pub async fn run_once_capture(&mut self, user_message: &str) -> Result<String> {
   23          self.add_message(
   24              Role::User,
   25              vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   26                  text: user_message.to_string(),
   27                  cache_control: None,
   28              }],
   29          );
   30          self.session.save()?;
   31          if trace_enabled() {
   32              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", self.session.id);
   33          }
   34          self.run_turn(false).await
   35      }
   36
   37      /// Run one conversation turn with streaming events via mpsc channel (per-client)
   38      pub async fn run_once_streaming_mpsc(
   39          &mut self,
   40          user_message: &str,
   41          images: Vec<(String, String)>,
   42          system_reminder: Option<String>,
   43          event_tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<ServerEvent>,
   44      ) -> Result<()> {
   45          // Inject any pending notifications before the user message
   46          let alerts = self.take_alerts();
   47          if !alerts.is_empty() {
   48              let alert_text = format!(
   49                  "[NOTIFICATION]\nYou received {} notification(s) from other agents working in this codebase:\n\n{}\n\nUse the communicate tool to coordinate with other agents (prefer dm; broadcast reaches only your spawned subtree).",
   50                  alerts.len(),
   51                  alerts.join("\n\n---\n\n")
   52              );
   53              self.add_message(
   54                  Role::User,
   55                  vec![ContentBlock::Text {
   56                      text: alert_text,
   57                      cache_control: None,
   58                  }],
   59              );
   60          }
   61
   62          self.current_turn_system_reminder =
   63              system_reminder.filter(|value| !value.trim().is_empty());
   64
   65          let mut blocks: Vec<ContentBlock> = images
   66              .into_iter()
   67              .map(|(media_type, data)| ContentBlock::Image { media_type, data })
   68              .collect();
   69          blocks.push(ContentBlock::Text {
   70              text: user_message.to_string(),
   71              cache_control: None,
   72          });
   73
   74          if blocks.len() > 1 {
   75              crate::logging::info(&format!(
   76                  "Agent received message with {} image(s)",
   77                  blocks.len() - 1
   78              ));
   79          }
   80
   81          self.add_message(Role::User, blocks);
   82          crate::telemetry::record_turn();
   83          self.session.save()?;
   84          let turn_started_at = Instant::now();
   85          let start_message_index = self.message_count();
   86          self.fire_turn_start_hook("chat");
   87          let result = self.run_turn_streaming_mpsc(event_tx).await;
   88          self.current_turn_system_reminder = None;
   89          self.fire_turn_end_hook(&result, turn_started_at, start_message_index);
   90          result
   91      }
   92
   93      /// Fire the `turn_start` observer hook when a turn begins, before the model
   94      /// starts generating (and before the first `pre_tool`). This lets external
   95      /// integrations (terminal multiplexers, status bars) detect that the agent
   96      /// is actively working during the otherwise-invisible window between prompt
   97      /// submission and the first tool call. No-op (without building the payload)
   98      /// when the hook is not configured.
   99      fn fire_turn_start_hook(&self, source: &str) {
  100          if !crate::hooks::hook_configured("turn_start") {
  101              return;
  102          }
  103          let mut event = crate::hooks::HookEvent::new("turn_start")
  104              .session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  105              .field("MODEL", self.provider_model())
  106              .field("SOURCE", source.to_string());

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 循环在每次请求前修复工具配对并重建稳定快照

SOURCE FACT

run_turn 注册 streaming/cancel guard；每圈先补齐缺失 tool outputs，再取得可能已压缩的 provider messages，锁定 tool definitions，取上一轮准备好的 memory，并构造静态/动态分离的 prompt。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

每次再问模型前先查账：工具有没有开单不回执、旧历史是否已折叠、这一轮工具箱和提示词是否稳定。

IMPLICATION

把异常历史修复、缓存稳定和请求生命周期放在一个明确关口。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 17–41
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 42–68
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   17      pub(super) async fn run_turn(&mut self, print_output: bool) -> Result<String> {
   18          self.set_log_context();
   19          crate::session_metrics::record_turn(&self.session.id);
   20          // Mark this session as actively streaming for presence UIs (e.g. the
   21          // macOS menu bar indicator). Cleared automatically on every exit path.
   22          let _streaming_guard = crate::session::StreamingGuard::new(self.session.id.clone());
   23          // Register this turn's cancel signal so session-level cancels reach
   24          // this in-flight turn even through stale control handles (issue #428).
   25          let _turn_cancel_guard = crate::turn_cancel_registry::register_active_turn(
   26              &self.session.id,
   27              self.graceful_shutdown.clone(),
   28          );
   29          let mut final_text = String::new();
   30          let trace = trace_enabled();
   31          let mut context_limit_retries = 0u32;
   32          let mut incomplete_continuations = 0u32;
   33          let mut empty_post_tool_continuations = 0u32;
   34
   35          loop {
   36              let repaired = self.repair_missing_tool_outputs();
   37              if repaired > 0 {
   38                  logging::warn(&format!(
   39                      "Recovered {} missing tool output(s) before API call",
   40                      repaired
   41                  ));
   42              }
   43              let (messages, compaction_event) = self.messages_for_provider();
   44              if let Some(event) = compaction_event {
   45                  // Reset cache tracker and tool lock on compaction since the message history changes
   46                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
   47                  self.locked_tools = None;
   48                  if print_output {
   49                      let tokens_str = event
   50                          .pre_tokens
   51                          .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
   52                          .unwrap_or_default();
   53                      crate::terminal_println!(
   54                          "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
   55                          event.trigger,
   56                          tokens_str
   57                      );
   58                  }
   59              }
   60
   61              let tools = self.tool_definitions().await;
   62              let messages: std::sync::Arc<[Message]> = messages.into();
   63              // Non-blocking memory: uses pending result from last turn, spawns check for next turn
   64              let memory_pending =
   65                  self.build_memory_prompt_nonblocking_shared(std::sync::Arc::clone(&messages), None);
   66              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   67              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   68              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);
   69
   70              // Check for client-side cache violations before memory injection.
   71              // Memory is an ephemeral suffix that changes each turn; tracking it would cause
   72              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   73              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);
   74
   75              // The request snapshot now owns everything the provider needs. Drop
   76              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.
   77              self.session.release_provider_messages_cache();
   78
   79              // Inject memory as a user message at the end (preserves cache prefix)
   80              let mut messages_with_memory: Vec<Message> = messages.iter().cloned().collect();
   81              if let Some(memory) = memory_pending.as_ref() {
   82                  let memory_count = memory.count.max(1);
   83                  let age_ms = memory.computed_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
   84                  crate::memory::record_injected_prompt(&memory.prompt, memory_count, age_ms);
   85                  self.record_memory_injection_in_session(memory);
   86                  logging::info(&format!(
   87                      "Memory injected as message ({} chars)",
   88                      memory.prompt.len()
   89                  ));
   90                  let (memory_msg, _persisted) = self.prepare_memory_injection_message(memory);
   91                  messages_with_memory.push(memory_msg);
   92              }
   93
   94              logging::info(&format!(
   95                  "API call starting: {} messages, {} tools",
   96                  messages_with_memory.len(),
   97                  tools.len()
   98              ));
   99              let api_start = Instant::now();
  100
  101              // Publish status for TUI to show during Task execution
  102              Bus::global().publish(BusEvent::SubagentStatus(SubagentStatus {
  103                  session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  104                  status: "calling API".to_string(),
  105                  model: Some(self.provider.model()),
  106              }));
  107
  108              let stamped = crate::config::config()
  109                  .features
  110                  .message_timestamps
  111                  .then(|| Message::with_timestamps(&messages_with_memory));
  112              let send_messages = stamped.as_deref().unwrap_or(&messages_with_memory);
  113              let prompt_has_recent_tool_result = Self::messages_end_with_tool_result(send_messages);
  114              self.last_status_detail = None;
  115              let mut stream = match self
  116                  .provider
  117                  .complete_split(
  118                      send_messages,
  119                      &tools,
  120                      &split_prompt.static_part,
  121                      &split_prompt.dynamic_part,
  122                      self.provider_session_id.as_deref(),
  123                  )
  124                  .await
  125              {
  126                  Ok(stream) => stream,
  127                  Err(e) => {
  128                      if self.try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&e.to_string()) {
  129                          context_limit_retries += 1;
  130                          if context_limit_retries > Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES {
  131                              logging::warn(
  132                                  "Context-limit compaction retry limit reached; giving up",
  133                              );
  134                              return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  135                                  "Context limit exceeded after {} compaction retries",
  136                                  Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES
  137                              ));
  138                          }
  139                          continue;

   17      const BATCH_NUDGE: &str = "<system-reminder>Several tool calls have been made one at a time.    19      fn update_sequential_tool_rounds(current: u32, tool_count: usize, used_batch: bool) -> u32 {
   20          if tool_count == 1 && !used_batch {
   21              current.saturating_add(1)
   22          } else {
   23              0
   24          }

   32          self.set_log_context();
   33          crate::session_metrics::record_turn(&self.session.id);
   34          // Mark this session as actively streaming for presence UIs (e.g. the
   35          // macOS menu bar indicator). Cleared automatically on every exit path.
   36          let _streaming_guard = crate::session::StreamingGuard::new(self.session.id.clone());
   37          // Register this turn's cancel signal so session-level cancels reach
   38          // this in-flight turn even through stale control handles (issue #428).

   40              &self.session.id,
   41              self.graceful_shutdown.clone(),
   42          );

   48          let mut fable_guardrail_reconsiderations = 0u32;
   49          let mut sequential_single_tool_rounds = 0u32;
   50          let mut batch_nudge_pending = false;
   51
   52          loop {

   54              // observed; the loop is re-entered by several recovery paths
   55              // (issue #732, regression of #428).

   64                      repaired
   65                  ));
   66              }

   71                  self.locked_tools = None;

   76                          .unwrap_or_default();

   78                          "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
   79                          event.trigger,
   80                          tokens_str
   81                      );
   82                  }
   83              }

   90              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   91              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   92              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);

   96              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   97              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);

  100              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.

  113                  ));

  115                  messages_with_memory.push(memory_msg);
  116              }

  120              ) {

  130              ));

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 中途断流先撤销半截状态再完整重播

SOURCE FACT

Provider 发出 RetryRollback 后，Agent 清空该次尝试的文本、tool calls、SDK results、reasoning 与 native compaction；Copilot runtime 仅在已向消费者发过可见输出且错误可重试时发送该事件。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

网络半路断了，不把前半句和前半个工具调用留在账上；先橡皮擦掉，再从头重放。

IMPLICATION

显著降低重试导致的重复文本和重复副作用；普通 stdout 无法擦除，只会显示断点标记。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 455–468
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 469–484
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 119301ac9d60 · open ledger commit ↗
crates/jcode-app-core/src/agent/turn_loops.rs:455–484PINNED ↗
  455                      StreamEvent::RetryRollback { attempt, max } => {
  456                          // Transient transport fault mid-stream; the provider is
  457                          // replaying the request. Discard this attempt's partial
  458                          // output so the replay doesn't duplicate it in history.
  459                          logging::warn(&format!(
  460                              "Mid-stream retry rollback (attempt {}/{}): discarding partial output ({} text chars, {} tool calls)",
  461                              attempt,
  462                              max,
  463                              text_content.len(),
  464                              tool_calls.len(),
  465                          ));
  466                          if print_output && !text_content.is_empty() {
  467                              // Already-printed text can't be unprinted on a plain
  468                              // stdout stream; mark the discontinuity instead.
  469                              println!("\n[connection interrupted, retrying response from the top]");
  470                              io::stdout().flush()?;
  471                          }
  472                          text_content.clear();
  473                          tool_calls.clear();
  474                          current_tool = None;
  475                          current_tool_input.clear();
  476                          sdk_tool_results.clear();
  477                          generated_image_contexts.clear();
  478                          reasoning_content.clear();
  479                          reasoning_signature.clear();
  480                          openai_reasoning_items.clear();
  481                          openai_native_compaction = None;
  482                          saw_message_end = false;
  483                          stop_reason = None;
  484                      }
  485                      StreamEvent::MessageEnd {
  486                          stop_reason: reason,
  487                      } => {
  488                          saw_message_end = true;
  489                          if reason.is_some() {
  490                              stop_reason = reason;
  491                          }
  492                          // Don't break yet - wait for SessionId which comes after MessageEnd
  493                          // (but stream close will also end the loop for providers without SessionId)
  494                      }
  495                      StreamEvent::SessionId(sid) => {
  496                          if trace {
  497                              eprintln!("[trace] session_id {}", sid);
  498                          }
  499                          self.provider_session_id = Some(sid.clone());
  500                          self.session.provider_session_id = Some(sid);
  501                          // We've received session_id, can exit the loop now
  502                          if saw_message_end {
  503                              break;
  504                          }
  505                      }
  506                      StreamEvent::UpstreamProvider { provider } => {
  507                          // Log upstream provider for local trace output
  508                          if trace {
  509                              eprintln!("[trace] upstream_provider={}", provider);
  510                          }
  511                          self.last_upstream_provider = Some(provider);
  512                      }
  513                      StreamEvent::OpenAIReasoning {
  514                          id,
  515                          summary,
  516                          encrypted_content,
  517                          status,
  518                      } => {
  519                          if store_reasoning_content {
  520                              openai_reasoning_items.push(ContentBlock::OpenAIReasoning {
  521                                  id,
  522                                  summary,
  523                                  encrypted_content,
  524                                  status,
  525                              });
  526                          }
  527                      }
  528                      StreamEvent::Compaction {
  529                          trigger,
  530                          pre_tokens,
  531                          openai_encrypted_content,
  532                      } => {
  533                          if let Some(encrypted_content) = openai_encrypted_content {
  534                              openai_native_compaction
  535                                  .get_or_insert((encrypted_content, self.session.messages.len()));
  536                          }
  537                          if print_output {
  538                              let tokens_str = pre_tokens
  539                                  .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
  540                                  .unwrap_or_default();
  541                              crate::terminal_println!(
  542                                  "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
  543                                  trigger,
  544                                  tokens_str
  545                              );
  546                          }
  547                      }
  548                      StreamEvent::NativeToolCall {
  549                          request_id,
  550                          tool_name,
  551                          input,
  552                      } => {

04
L1factarchitecture

04 · 上下文、截断回复和工具后空回复各有独立止损上限

SOURCE FACT

上下文自动压缩重试最多 5 次，不完整回复 continuation 最多 3 次，工具结果后的空白 final 最多 5 次；工具后空白会写入一条明确要求给 final answer 的 user message。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

不同故障不共用一个模糊的“最多重试 N 次”：箱子装不下、回答被截断、工具后失语分别计数。

IMPLICATION

长任务韧性好，也避免无限循环；自动补写的 user message会成为真实历史。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 5–9
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 10–15
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   17      pub(super) async fn run_turn(&mut self, print_output: bool) -> Result<String> {
   18          self.set_log_context();
   19          crate::session_metrics::record_turn(&self.session.id);
   20          // Mark this session as actively streaming for presence UIs (e.g. the
   21          // macOS menu bar indicator). Cleared automatically on every exit path.
   22          let _streaming_guard = crate::session::StreamingGuard::new(self.session.id.clone());
   23          // Register this turn's cancel signal so session-level cancels reach
   24          // this in-flight turn even through stale control handles (issue #428).
   25          let _turn_cancel_guard = crate::turn_cancel_registry::register_active_turn(
   26              &self.session.id,
   27              self.graceful_shutdown.clone(),
   28          );

   30          let trace = trace_enabled();
   31          let mut context_limit_retries = 0u32;
   32          let mut incomplete_continuations = 0u32;
   33          let mut empty_post_tool_continuations = 0u32;
   34
   35          loop {
   36              let repaired = self.repair_missing_tool_outputs();
   37              if repaired > 0 {
   38                  logging::warn(&format!(
   39                      "Recovered {} missing tool output(s) before API call",
   40                      repaired
   41                  ));
   42              }
   43              let (messages, compaction_event) = self.messages_for_provider();

   45                  // Reset cache tracker and tool lock on compaction since the message history changes
   46                  self.cache_tracker.reset();
   47                  self.locked_tools = None;

   49                      let tokens_str = event
   50                          .pre_tokens
   51                          .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
   52                          .unwrap_or_default();
   53                      crate::terminal_println!(
   54                          "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
   55                          event.trigger,
   56                          tokens_str
   57                      );
   58                  }
   59              }
   60
   61              let tools = self.tool_definitions().await;
   62              let messages: std::sync::Arc<[Message]> = messages.into();
   63              // Non-blocking memory: uses pending result from last turn, spawns check for next turn
   64              let memory_pending =
   65                  self.build_memory_prompt_nonblocking_shared(std::sync::Arc::clone(&messages), None);
   66              // Use split prompt for better caching - static content cached, dynamic not
   67              let split_prompt = self.build_system_prompt_split(None);
   68              self.log_prompt_prefix_accounting(&split_prompt, &tools);
   69
   70              // Check for client-side cache violations before memory injection.
   71              // Memory is an ephemeral suffix that changes each turn; tracking it would cause
   72              // false-positive violations every turn (prior turn's memory ≠ current history prefix).
   73              self.record_client_cache_request(&messages);
   74
   75              // The request snapshot now owns everything the provider needs. Drop
   76              // the session's derived transcript copy before the network wait.
   77              self.session.release_provider_messages_cache();
   78
   79              // Inject memory as a user message at the end (preserves cache prefix)
   80              let mut messages_with_memory: Vec<Message> = messages.iter().cloned().collect();

   83                  let age_ms = memory.computed_at.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
   84                  crate::memory::record_injected_prompt(&memory.prompt, memory_count, age_ms);
   85                  self.record_memory_injection_in_session(memory);

DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

Model & context

9 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L2factmodelContext

01 · Provider 契约不仅抽象生成，还抽象认证、路由、transport 与原生能力

SOURCE FACT

Provider trait 提供 complete/complete_split、模型/route/effort/tier/transport 切换、认证解析、context window、fork、native tool result sender 和 native compaction；fork 明确要求独立可变状态。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

它不是只把 URL 换掉；连“谁付费、用哪条线路、能不能续上服务端会话、工具由谁执行、压缩由谁做”都在同一接口里。

IMPLICATION

多后端能力完整，但 trait 面积很大，新 Provider 的一致性测试成本高。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 76–100
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 101–126
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

   76  pub trait Provider: Send + Sync {
   77      /// Send messages and get a streaming response.
   78      /// resume_session_id: Optional session ID to resume a previous conversation (provider-specific).
   79      async fn complete(
   80          &self,
   81          messages: &[Message],
   82          tools: &[ToolDefinition],
   83          system: &str,
   84          resume_session_id: Option<&str>,
   85      ) -> Result<EventStream>;
   86
   87      /// Send messages with split system prompt for better caching.
   88      async fn complete_split(
   89          &self,
   90          messages: &[Message],
   91          tools: &[ToolDefinition],
   92          system_static: &str,
   93          system_dynamic: &str,
   94          resume_session_id: Option<&str>,
   95      ) -> Result<EventStream> {
   96          let dynamic_messages = messages_with_dynamic_system_context(messages, system_dynamic);
   97          self.complete(&dynamic_messages, tools, system_static, resume_session_id)
   98              .await
   99      }
  100
  101      /// Get the provider name.
  102      ///
  103      /// This is the stable, machine-facing identifier (e.g. `"openrouter"`,
  104      /// `"claude"`). Several surfaces key billing and routing decisions off this
  105      /// value, so it must stay constant for a given provider class even when the
  106      /// underlying runtime is a specific OpenAI-compatible profile. Use
  107      /// [`Provider::display_name`] for anything shown to the user.
  108      fn name(&self) -> &str;
  109
  110      /// Human-facing provider label for the *current runtime selection*.
  111      ///
  112      /// Defaults to [`Provider::name`]. Provider orchestrators that multiplex
  113      /// several backends behind one `name()` (notably the OpenRouter slot, which
  114      /// also serves direct OpenAI-compatible profiles such as NVIDIA NIM or
  115      /// DeepSeek) override this so the UI reflects the profile the user actually
  116      /// selected at runtime instead of a fixed aggregator label.
  117      fn display_name(&self) -> String {
  118          self.name().to_string()
  119      }
  120
  121      /// Get the model identifier being used.
  122      fn model(&self) -> String {
  123          "unknown".to_string()
  124      }
  125
  126      /// Human-readable description of the auth method the active provider will
  127      /// actually use for the next request (e.g. "OAuth" or "API key"), or `None`
  128      /// when there is no meaningful OAuth-vs-API-key distinction. UI surfaces use
  129      /// this to report the auth method accurately instead of inferring it from
  130      /// which credentials happen to be configured.
  131      fn active_auth_method_label(&self) -> Option<&'static str> {
  132          self.active_resolved_credential()
  133              .map(ResolvedCredential::auth_method_label)
  134      }
  135
  136      /// The credential the active provider will actually use for the next
  137      /// request, when the provider supports both OAuth and API-key auth
  138      /// (currently Anthropic and OpenAI). Returns `None` for providers with no
  139      /// OAuth-vs-API-key ambiguity.
  140      ///
  141      /// This is the authoritative, server-side answer to "subscription or
  142      /// cost-based billing?". It is computed from the provider's live credential
  143      /// mode rather than re-derived from credential probes or env strings, so
  144      /// every surface (header tag, info-widget usage, model-switch line) and
  145      /// every transport (local or remote) agrees. Remote clients receive the
  146      /// resolved value over the wire instead of guessing from a provider name.
  147      fn active_resolved_credential(&self) -> Option<ResolvedCredential> {
  148          None
  149      }
  150
  151      /// The credential the active dual-auth provider (Anthropic / OpenAI) will
  152      /// use *when the user has explicitly pinned one* (OAuth or API key), without
  153      /// resolving "auto".
  154      ///
  155      /// Unlike [`Provider::active_resolved_credential`], this never touches disk
  156      /// or env to resolve an auto/default choice: it returns `Some` only for an
  157      /// explicit in-memory pin and `None` for auto mode (or providers with no
  158      /// OAuth-vs-API-key ambiguity). UI surfaces that rebuild every frame (the
  159      /// info widget) use it to reflect an explicit OAuth<->API switch instantly
  160      /// while leaving the cheap cached heuristic to handle the auto case.
  161      fn active_explicit_credential(&self) -> Option<ResolvedCredential> {
  162          None
  163      }
  164

  166      fn supports_image_input(&self) -> bool {
  167          false
  168      }
  169
  170      /// Set the model to use (returns error if model not supported).
  171      fn set_model(&self, _model: &str) -> Result<()> {
  172          Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  173              "This provider does not support model switching"
  174          ))
  175      }
  176
  177      /// Select a structured model route.
  178      ///
  179      /// Most single-runtime providers can treat this as `set_model(model)`. Provider
  180      /// orchestrators should override this to activate the exact runtime identified
  181      /// by [`RouteSelection::runtime_key`] instead of reparsing a lossy model string.
  182      fn set_route_selection(&self, selection: &RouteSelection) -> Result<()> {
  183          self.set_model(&selection.routed_model_spec())
  184      }

L1factmodelContext

02 · MultiProvider 同时容纳九类 runtime 与兼容端点 profile

SOURCE FACT

MultiProvider 持有 Claude CLI、Anthropic API、OpenAI、Copilot、Antigravity、Gemini、Cursor、Bedrock、OpenRouter，以及独立 keyed OpenAI-compatible profiles；active provider 可热切换。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

像一个总机：同一 Agent 可以接订阅 CLI、官方 API、Copilot、Gemini、Bedrock 或自定义兼容网关，并保留各自认证身份。

IMPLICATION

模型路由覆盖面极广；OpenRouter 槽位兼容多 profile，代码中有大量防止 profile 身份丢失的分支。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 328–350
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 351–374
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  329  pub struct MultiProvider {

  331      claude: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,

  334      openai: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  335      /// GitHub Copilot API provider (direct API, hot-swappable after login).
  336      /// Held as `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  337      /// `jcode-provider-copilot-runtime` and is instantiated through
  338      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  339      copilot_api: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  340      /// Antigravity provider (direct HTTPS, hot-swappable after login). Held as
  341      /// `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  342      /// `jcode-provider-antigravity-runtime` and is instantiated through
  343      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  344      antigravity: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  345      /// Gemini provider (hot-swappable after login). Held as `dyn Provider`:
  346      /// the concrete runtime lives downstream in `jcode-provider-gemini-runtime`
  347      /// and is instantiated through `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  348      gemini: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  349      /// Cursor provider (native/direct API, hot-swappable after login). Held as
  350      /// `dyn Provider`: the concrete runtime lives downstream in
  351      /// `jcode-provider-cursor-runtime` and is instantiated through
  352      /// `external::instantiate_external_provider`.
  353      cursor: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  354      /// AWS Bedrock provider (native Converse/ConverseStream, IAM/SigV4)
  355      bedrock: RwLock<Option<Arc<bedrock::BedrockProvider>>>,
  356      /// OpenRouter API provider
  357      openrouter: RwLock<Option<Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  358      /// Direct OpenAI-compatible runtimes keyed by profile id.
  359      ///
  360      /// These use the same wire protocol implementation as OpenRouter, but must
  361      /// not occupy the real OpenRouter slot. Keeping them separate prevents a
  362      /// compatible endpoint selection from corrupting later OpenRouter model
  363      /// switches, catalog display, or auth refresh handling.
  364      openai_compatible_profiles: RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn Provider>>>,
  365      active_openai_compatible_profile: RwLock<Option<String>>,
  366      active: RwLock<ActiveProvider>,
  367      /// Use Claude CLI instead of direct API (legacy mode)
  368      use_claude_cli: bool,
  369      /// Notifications generated during provider/account auto-selection.
  370      /// The TUI should drain and display these on session start.
  371      startup_notices: RwLock<Vec<String>>,
  372      /// CLI/environment selection to use when creating fresh sessions. This is
  373      /// only an initial preference and never restricts later model switches.
  374      initial_provider: Option<ActiveProvider>,
  375      /// Short-TTL memo for the full route-catalog build.
  376      ///
  377      /// Building the catalog is expensive (per-route pricing lookups, endpoint
  378      /// cache reads, credential probes) and the shared server rebuilds it for
  379      /// every connection whenever a `ModelsUpdated` bus event fans out. During
  380      /// a burst of client spawns that multiplied into hundreds of builds within
  381      /// a couple of seconds, saturating every core. The memo collapses those
  382      /// into one build per TTL window; auth/model changes invalidate it
  383      /// explicitly so pickers never see stale routes after a switch.
  384      routes_memo: Mutex<Option<RoutesMemoEntry>>,
  385      /// Number of model-catalog prefetches launched by the latest auth refresh.
  386      /// Shared by forks so the server can wait for real work rather than sleeping
  387      /// through a fixed quiet period after login.
  388      post_auth_refreshes_pending: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize>,
  389  }
  390
  391  /// Memoized route catalog with the inputs that decide its freshness: build
  392  /// time (short TTL), the auth generation at build time (bumped by
  393  /// `AuthStatus::invalidate_cache()` on login/logout/credential edits), and the
  394  /// catalog generation (bumped by prefetch/refresh completions).
  395  #[derive(Clone)]
  396  struct RoutesMemoEntry {
  397      built_at: std::time::Instant,
  398      auth_generation: u64,
  399      catalog_generation: u64,
  400      routes: Vec<ModelRoute>,
  401      /// `listable_model_names_from_routes(&routes)`, cached because the
  402      /// non-chat-model heuristic string-scans every route name and callers
  403      /// (catalog snapshots) ask for names and routes together.
  404      listable_models: Vec<String>,
  405  }
  406
  407  /// Process-wide route-catalog memo shared across `MultiProvider` instances.
  408  ///
  409  /// The shared server forks one `MultiProvider` per client connection, so a
  410  /// per-instance memo cannot deduplicate the builds triggered by a burst of
  411  /// simultaneous client spawns: every fresh fork still built its own catalog.
  412  /// Catalog content is derived almost entirely from process-global state
  413  /// (credential files, disk caches, config), so identical forks can share one
  414  /// build. Instance-specific inputs (active provider/model/profile) are folded
  415  /// into the memo key; anything not captured is bounded by the short TTL and
  416  /// the auth/catalog generations.
  417  static GLOBAL_ROUTES_MEMO: LazyLock<Mutex<HashMap<String, RoutesMemoEntry>>> =
  418      LazyLock::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
  419
  420  /// Single-flight guard for catalog builds. During a client connect burst every
  421  /// connection calls `model_routes()` at nearly the same instant; without this
  422  /// they all miss the still-empty memo and build the same catalog in parallel
  423  /// (a thundering herd that pegs every core). Holding this lock across the
  424  /// build makes followers block (sleep, not spin) until the leader publishes
  425  /// its result, which they then serve from the shared memo.
  426  static GLOBAL_ROUTES_BUILD_LOCK: Mutex<()> = Mutex::new(());
  427
  428  /// Bumped whenever provider catalogs change out-of-band (prefetch completion,
  429  /// forced catalog refresh, auth changes). Invalidates every shared memo entry.
  430  static CATALOG_GENERATION: std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64::new(0);
  431
  432  fn catalog_generation() -> u64 {
  433      CATALOG_GENERATION.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)
  434  }
  435
  436  /// Invalidate every memoized route catalog in the process.
  437  ///
  438  /// Called when provider catalogs change out-of-band (background catalog
  439  /// refresh completion, auth changes). This is what keeps the route memo TTL a
  440  /// backstop rather than the mechanism: a completed refresh is reflected on the
  441  /// next render instead of waiting for the memo to expire.
  442  pub fn bump_catalog_generation() {
  443      CATALOG_GENERATION.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
  444  }
  445

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 服务端会话、原生工具和原生压缩都能穿过统一事件流

SOURCE FACT

StreamEvent::SessionId 会保存 provider resumable session；Compaction 可保存 OpenAI encrypted content；NativeToolCall 经本地 registry 执行后通过 provider native_result_sender 回传 SDK bridge。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

底层 Provider 可以说“以后用这个会话号续聊”“我已经压缩过了”或“替我本地跑这个工具”，上层都能接住。

IMPLICATION

兼容 provider-native agent runtime，而非强迫所有后端退化成纯文本 function calling。

LINE-BY-LINE READING

    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 516–547
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  553                          // Execute native tool and send result back to SDK bridge
  554                          if trace {

  557                                  request_id, tool_name

  566                              graceful_shutdown_signal: Some(self.graceful_shutdown.clone()),
  567                              execution_mode: ToolExecutionMode::AgentTurn,
  568                          };
  569                          crate::telemetry::record_tool_call();
  570                          let tool_result = self
  571                              .registry
  572                              .execute(&tool_name, ToolCall::normalize_input_to_object(input), ctx)
  573                              .await;
  574                          if tool_result.is_err() {
  575                              crate::telemetry::record_tool_failure();
  576                          }
  577                          let native_result = match tool_result {
  578                              Ok(output) => NativeToolResult::success(request_id, output.output),
  579                              Err(e) => NativeToolResult::error(request_id, e.to_string()),
  580                          };
  581                          // Send result back to SDK bridge
  582                          if let Some(sender) = self.provider.native_result_sender() {
  583                              let _ = sender.send(native_result).await;
  584                          }
  585                      }

  589                      } => {

  591                              eprintln!("[trace] stream_error {}", message);
  592                          }
  593                          if self.try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&message) {
  594                              log_agent_provider_stream_lifecycle(
  595                                  logging::LogLevel::Warn,
  596                                  self,
  597                                  "stream_event_retry_after_compaction",
  598                                  api_start,

  601                                      ("error", message.clone()),
  602                                      (
  603                                          "context_limit_retries",
  604                                          (context_limit_retries + 1).to_string(),
  605                                      ),
  606                                  ],
  607                              );
  608                              context_limit_retries += 1;
  609                              if context_limit_retries > Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES {
  610                                  logging::warn(
  611                                      "Context-limit compaction retry limit reached; giving up",
  612                                  );
  613                                  return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
  614                                      "Context limit exceeded after {} compaction retries",
  615                                      Self::MAX_CONTEXT_LIMIT_RETRIES
  616                                  ));
  617                              }
  618                              retry_after_compaction = true;

  570                              let tokens_str = pre_tokens
  571                                  .map(|t| format!(" ({} tokens)", t))
  572                                  .unwrap_or_default();
  573                              crate::terminal_println!(
  574                                  "📦 Context compacted ({}){}",
  575                                  trigger,
  576                                  tokens_str
  577                              );
  578                          }
  579                      }
  580                      StreamEvent::NativeToolCall {
  581                          request_id,
  582                          tool_name,
  583                          input,
  584                      } => {
  585                          // Execute native tool and send result back to SDK bridge
  586                          if trace {
  587                              eprintln!(
  588                                  "[trace] native_tool_call request_id={} tool={}",
  589                                  request_id, tool_name
  590                              );
  591                          }
  592                          let ctx = ToolContext {
  593                              session_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  594                              message_id: self.session.id.clone(),
  595                              tool_call_id: request_id.clone(),
  596                              working_dir: self.working_dir().map(PathBuf::from),
  597                              stdin_request_tx: self.stdin_request_tx.clone(),
  598                              graceful_shutdown_signal: Some(self.graceful_shutdown.clone()),
  599                              execution_mode: ToolExecutionMode::AgentTurn,
  600                          };
  601                          crate::telemetry::record_tool_call();
  602                          let tool_result = self
  603                              .registry
  604                              .execute(&tool_name, ToolCall::normalize_input_to_object(input), ctx)
  605                              .await;
  606                          if tool_result.is_err() {
  607                              crate::telemetry::record_tool_failure();
  608                          }
  609                          let native_result = match tool_result {
  610                              Ok(output) => NativeToolResult::success(request_id, output.output),
  611                              Err(e) => NativeToolResult::error(request_id, e.to_string()),
  612                          };
  613                          // Send result back to SDK bridge
  614                          if let Some(sender) = self.provider.native_result_sender() {
  615                              let _ = sender.send(native_result).await;
  616                          }
  617                      }
  618                      StreamEvent::Error {
04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 静态前缀与每轮动态上下文分离，memory 放尾部保缓存

SOURCE FACT

system prompt 把 base prompt、AGENTS、overlay、preferred-tools、skills 放 static_part；memory、active skill、reminder 与 swarm effort 放 dynamic_part。run_turn 在 client cache 检查后把 memory 作为末尾 user message注入。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

不常变的说明书放书脊，记忆和本轮提醒贴在最后一页；这样改便签不会让整本书的缓存失效。

IMPLICATION

明显以 prompt-cache economics 为一等设计目标。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 451–503
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 504–557
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  451  /// Build system prompt split into static (cacheable) and dynamic parts
  452  /// This improves cache hit rate by keeping frequently-changing content separate
  453  pub fn build_system_prompt_split(
  454      skill_prompt: Option<&str>,
  455      available_skills: &[SkillInfo],
  456      is_selfdev: bool,
  457      memory_prompt: Option<&str>,
  458      working_dir: Option<&Path>,
  459  ) -> (SplitSystemPrompt, ContextInfo) {
  460      build_system_prompt_split_with_capabilities(
  461          skill_prompt,
  462          available_skills,
  463          is_selfdev,
  464          memory_prompt,
  465          working_dir,
  466          PromptCapabilities::current(),
  467      )
  468  }
  469
  470  pub fn build_system_prompt_split_with_capabilities(
  471      skill_prompt: Option<&str>,
  472      available_skills: &[SkillInfo],
  473      is_selfdev: bool,
  474      memory_prompt: Option<&str>,
  475      working_dir: Option<&Path>,
  476      capabilities: PromptCapabilities,
  477  ) -> (SplitSystemPrompt, ContextInfo) {
  478      let mut static_parts = base_system_prompt_parts(capabilities);
  479      let mut dynamic_parts = Vec::new();
  480      let mut info = ContextInfo {
  481          system_prompt_chars: static_parts.join("\n\n").len(),
  482          ..Default::default()
  483      };
  484
  485      // === STATIC CONTENT (cacheable) ===
  486
  487      // Add self-dev guidance only in active self-dev sessions. Normal sessions
  488      // learn about the on-ramp from the mode-aware `selfdev` tool schema.
  489      if is_selfdev {
  490          let selfdev_prompt = build_selfdev_prompt_static_for_working_dir(working_dir);
  491          info.selfdev_chars = selfdev_prompt.len();
  492          static_parts.push(selfdev_prompt);
  493      }
  494
  495      // Add AGENTS.md instructions (static per project)
  496      let (md_content, md_info) = load_agents_md_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  497      if let Some(content) = md_content {
  498          static_parts.push(content);
  499      }
  500      info.has_project_agents_md = md_info.has_project_agents_md;
  501      info.project_agents_md_chars = md_info.project_agents_md_chars;
  502      info.has_global_agents_md = md_info.has_global_agents_md;
  503      info.global_agents_md_chars = md_info.global_agents_md_chars;
  504
  505      // Add optional prompt overlays from ~/.jcode/ and ./.jcode/
  506      let (overlay_content, overlay_chars) = load_prompt_overlay_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  507      if let Some(content) = overlay_content {
  508          info.prompt_overlay_chars = overlay_chars;
  509          static_parts.push(content);
  510      }
  511
  512      // Add optional preferred-tool guidance (static per project/user)
  513      let (preferred_tools_content, preferred_tools_chars) =
  514          load_preferred_tools_files_from_dir(working_dir);
  515      if let Some(content) = preferred_tools_content {
  516          info.preferred_tools_chars = preferred_tools_chars;
  517          static_parts.push(content);
  518      }
  519
  520      // Add available skills list (fairly static)
  521      if !available_skills.is_empty() {
  522          let mut skills_section = "# Available Skills\n\nYou have access to the following skills that the user can invoke with `/skillname`:\n".to_string();
  523          for skill in available_skills {
  524              skills_section.push_str(&format!("\n- `/{} ` - {}", skill.name, skill.description));
  525          }
  526          skills_section.push_str(
  527              "\n\nWhen a user asks about available skills or capabilities, mention these skills.",
  528          );
  529          info.skills_chars = skills_section.len();
  530          static_parts.push(skills_section);
  531      }
  532
  533      // === TURN CONTEXT (not cached) ===
  534
  535      // Memory prompt (changes per conversation)
  536      if let Some(memory) = memory_prompt {
  537          info.memory_chars = memory.len();
  538          dynamic_parts.push(memory.to_string());
  539      }
  540
  541      // Active skill prompt (changes per skill invocation)
  542      if let Some(skill) = skill_prompt {
  543          dynamic_parts.push(format!("# Active Skill\n\n{}", skill));
  544      }
  545
  546      let static_part = static_parts.join("\n\n");
  547      let dynamic_part = dynamic_parts.join("\n\n");
  548      info.total_chars = static_part.len() + dynamic_part.len();
  549
  550      (
  551          SplitSystemPrompt {
  552              static_part,
  553              dynamic_part,
  554          },
  555          info,
  556      )
  557  }

05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 压缩器不复制历史，只记录被摘要的前缀游标

SOURCE FACT

CompactionManager 不拥有 messages，只存 compacted_count、active_summary、active suffix char estimate 与 pending background task；恢复旧会话时先抑制压缩，直到真正的新消息加入。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

原始账本仍归 Session 管，压缩器只记“前 N 条已折叠成摘要”，不会偷偷维护第二份容易漂移的历史。

IMPLICATION

所有权清楚且便于 rewind；游标、摘要和 session persistence 必须原子同步。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 128–165
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 166–205
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 119301ac9d60 · open ledger commit ↗
crates/jcode-base/src/compaction.rs:128–205PINNED ↗
  128  /// Manages background compaction of conversation context.
  129  ///
  130  /// Does NOT own message data. The caller owns the messages and passes
  131  /// references into methods that need them. After compaction, the manager
  132  /// records `compacted_count` — the number of leading messages that have
  133  /// been summarized and should be skipped when building API payloads.
  134  pub struct CompactionManager {
  135      /// Number of leading messages that have been compacted into the summary.
  136      /// When building API messages, skip the first `compacted_count` messages.
  137      compacted_count: usize,
  138
  139      /// Active summary (if we've compacted before)
  140      active_summary: Option<Summary>,
  141
  142      /// Rolling char estimate for the active (non-compacted) message suffix.
  143      ///
  144      /// In the common append-only case this is maintained incrementally, so token
  145      /// estimation does not need to rescan the entire active history every time.
  146      /// Bundled with its own dirty flag so the value and staleness can never
  147      /// drift apart (see [`ActiveCharEstimate`]).
  148      active_chars: ActiveCharEstimate,
  149
  150      /// Background compaction task handle
  151      pending_task: Option<JoinHandle<Result<CompactionResult>>>,
  152
  153      /// User-facing trigger label for the currently running background compaction.
  154      pending_trigger: Option<String>,
  155
  156      /// Turn index (relative to uncompacted messages) where pending compaction will cut off
  157      pending_cutoff: usize,
  158
  159      /// Total turns seen (for tracking)
  160      total_turns: usize,
  161
  162      /// When true, session restore/reseed has just loaded old history and
  163      /// compaction must stay disabled until a genuinely new message is added.
  164      suppress_compaction_until_new_message: bool,
  165
  166      /// Token budget
  167      token_budget: usize,
  168
  169      /// Provider-reported input token usage from the latest request.
  170      /// Used to trigger compaction with real token counts instead of only heuristics.
  171      observed_input_tokens: Option<u64>,
  172
  173      /// Last compaction event (if any)
  174      last_compaction: Option<CompactionEvent>,
  175
  176      // ── Mode & strategy ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  177      /// Active compaction mode (set from config at construction)
  178      mode: crate::config::CompactionMode,
  179
  180      /// Config snapshot for mode-specific parameters
  181      compaction_config: crate::config::CompactionConfig,
  182
  183      // ── Proactive mode state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  184      /// Rolling window of observed token counts, one entry per turn snapshot.
  185      /// Used to compute EWMA growth rate for proactive compaction.
  186      token_history: VecDeque<u64>,
  187
  188      /// Total turns elapsed since the last successful compaction.
  189      /// Used as a cooldown anti-signal.
  190      turns_since_last_compact: usize,
  191
  192      // ── Semantic mode state ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  193      /// Per-turn embedding snapshots for topic-shift detection.
  194      /// Each entry is the L2-normalized embedding of the last assistant message
  195      /// of that turn (truncated to EMBED_MAX_CHARS_PER_MSG for speed).
  196      embedding_history: VecDeque<Vec<f32>>,
  197
  198      /// Local cache for semantic compaction embeddings keyed by truncated-text hash.
  199      /// Stores both successful embeddings and failed lookups (`None`) so repeated
  200      /// semantic scans do not redo the same work.
  201      semantic_embed_cache: HashMap<u64, (Option<Vec<f32>>, u64)>,
  202
  203      /// Monotonic recency counter for the semantic embedding cache LRU.
  204      semantic_embed_cache_counter: u64,
  205  }
  206
  207  impl CompactionManager {
  208      pub fn new() -> Self {
  209          let cfg = crate::config::config().compaction.clone();
  210          let mode = cfg.mode.clone();
  211          Self {
  212              compacted_count: 0,
  213              active_summary: None,

  230          }
  231      }
  232
  233      /// Reset all compaction state

  235          *self = Self::new();
  236      }

  239          self.token_budget = budget;
  240          self

  246      }

  253      /// Notify the manager that a message was added.
  254      ///
  255      /// Legacy callers that do not provide the message content keep turn counts
  256      /// correct, but mark the rolling char estimate dirty so the next token
  257      /// estimate will resync from the provided history slice.
  258      pub fn notify_message_added(&mut self) {
  259          self.total_turns += 1;
  260          self.suppress_compaction_until_new_message = false;
  261          self.active_chars.invalidate();
  262      }
  263
  264      /// Notify the manager that a message was added and update the rolling char
  265      /// estimate incrementally.
  266      pub fn notify_message_added_with(&mut self, message: &Message) {
  267          self.notify_message_added_blocks(&message.content);
  268      }
  269
  270      pub fn notify_message_added_blocks(&mut self, content: &[ContentBlock]) {
  271          self.total_turns += 1;
  272          self.suppress_compaction_until_new_message = false;
  273          self.active_chars.append_exact(content_char_count(content));
  274      }

L1factmodelContext

06 · 压缩支持 reactive、趋势预测和语义换题三种策略

SOURCE FACT

reactive 在固定阈值触发；proactive 用 token history 的 EWMA 增长投影；semantic 用 turn embeddings 检测 topic shift，无 embedding 时回退 proactive。统一 anti-signals 防止重复、过早、停滞期和 cooldown 内压缩。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

可以等箱子快满再整理，也可以看增长速度提前整理，还可以在话题换挡时整理；但都会先检查“现在整理是否值得”。

IMPLICATION

上下文策略比多数单摘要 Agent 丰富，语义模式依赖本地 embedding 可用性和阈值调优。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 456–498
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 499–543
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

crates/jcode-base/src/compaction.rs:456–543PINNED ↗
  456      // ── Anti-signal guard (shared by proactive + semantic) ──────────────────
  457
  458      /// Returns `true` when any anti-signal fires and we should NOT compact
  459      /// proactively right now.
  460      ///
  461      /// Anti-signals are universal guards applied before the mode-specific
  462      /// trigger logic. They prevent wasted work and respect user intent.
  463      fn anti_signals_block(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  464          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  465
  466          // 1. Already compacting — never double-trigger.
  467          if self.pending_task.is_some() {
  468              return true;
  469          }
  470
  471          // 2. Context below the proactive floor — too early regardless of trend.
  472          let usage = self.context_usage_with(all_messages);
  473          if usage < cfg.proactive_floor {
  474              return true;
  475          }
  476
  477          // 3. Not enough token history to project from.
  478          if self.token_history.len() < cfg.min_samples {
  479              return true;
  480          }
  481
  482          // 4. Growth has stalled: last stall_window snapshots show no increase.
  483          //    If tokens haven't grown, there's no urgency.
  484          if self.token_history.len() >= cfg.stall_window {
  485              let recent: Vec<u64> = self
  486                  .token_history
  487                  .iter()
  488                  .rev()
  489                  .take(cfg.stall_window)
  490                  .cloned()
  491                  .collect();
  492              let oldest = recent[recent.len() - 1];
  493              let newest = recent[0];
  494              if newest <= oldest {
  495                  return true;
  496              }
  497          }
  498
  499          // 5. Cooldown: too soon after the last compaction.
  500          if self.turns_since_last_compact < cfg.min_turns_between_compactions {
  501              return true;
  502          }
  503
  504          false
  505      }
  506
  507      // ── Proactive mode trigger ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  508
  509      /// Returns `true` if the proactive strategy thinks we should compact now.
  510      ///
  511      /// Uses an EWMA over the token history to project forward `lookahead_turns`
  512      /// turns. If the projected token count would exceed the 80% threshold,
  513      /// it's time to compact before we get there.
  514      fn should_compact_proactively(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  515          if self.anti_signals_block(all_messages) {
  516              return false;
  517          }
  518
  519          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  520          let budget = self.token_budget as f64;
  521          let threshold = COMPACTION_THRESHOLD as f64 * budget;
  522
  523          // Compute EWMA of per-turn token deltas.
  524          // We need at least 2 snapshots to get a delta.
  525          let snapshots: Vec<u64> = self.token_history.iter().cloned().collect();
  526          if snapshots.len() < 2 {
  527              return false;
  528          }
  529
  530          let alpha = cfg.ewma_alpha as f64;
  531          let mut ewma_delta: f64 = (snapshots[1] as f64) - (snapshots[0] as f64);
  532          ewma_delta = ewma_delta.max(0.0);
  533          for i in 2..snapshots.len() {
  534              let delta = ((snapshots[i] as f64) - (snapshots[i - 1] as f64)).max(0.0);
  535              ewma_delta = alpha * delta + (1.0 - alpha) * ewma_delta;
  536          }
  537          let Some(current) = snapshots.last().copied().map(|value| value as f64) else {
  538              return false;
  539          };
  540          let projected = current + ewma_delta * cfg.lookahead_turns as f64;
  541
  542          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  543              "[compaction/proactive] current={:.0} ewma_delta={:.1}/turn projected@{}turns={:.0} threshold={:.0}",
  544              current, ewma_delta, cfg.lookahead_turns, projected, threshold
  545          ));
  546
  547          projected >= threshold
  548      }
  549
  550      // ── Semantic mode trigger ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  551
  552      /// Returns `true` if the semantic strategy detects a topic shift or
  553      /// predicts we should compact now.
  554      ///
  555      /// Topic-shift detection: compares the mean embedding of the oldest half
  556      /// of the history window against the newest half. A low cosine similarity
  557      /// between the two clusters indicates a topic boundary was crossed —
  558      /// the previous topic is complete and safe to summarize.
  559      ///
  560      /// Falls back to proactive logic if embeddings are unavailable.
  561      fn should_compact_semantic(&self, all_messages: &[Message]) -> bool {
  562          if self.anti_signals_block(all_messages) {
  563              return false;
  564          }
  565
  566          // Need enough embedding history to split into two halves.
  567          let history_len = self.embedding_history.len();
  568          if history_len < 4 {
  569              // Fall back to proactive trigger.
  570              return self.should_compact_proactively(all_messages);
  571          }
  572
  573          let cfg = &self.compaction_config;
  574          let half = history_len / 2;
  575
  576          let old_embeddings: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = self.embedding_history.iter().take(half).collect();
  577          let new_embeddings: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = self.embedding_history.iter().skip(half).collect();
  578
  579          let dim = old_embeddings[0].len();
  580
  581          // Compute mean embedding for each half.
  582          let mean_old = mean_embedding(&old_embeddings, dim);
  583          let mean_new = mean_embedding(&new_embeddings, dim);
  584
  585          let similarity = crate::embedding::cosine_similarity(&mean_old, &mean_new);
  586
  587          crate::logging::info(&format!(
  588              "[compaction/semantic] topic similarity (old vs new half) = {:.3} (threshold={:.2})",
  589              similarity, cfg.topic_shift_threshold
  590          ));
  591
  592          if similarity < cfg.topic_shift_threshold {
  593              crate::logging::info(
  594                  "[compaction/semantic] Topic shift detected — triggering proactive compaction",
  595              );
  596              return true;
  597          }
  598
  599          // No topic shift — still fall back to proactive growth check.
  600          self.should_compact_proactively(all_messages)
  601      }
  602
  603      /// Build a relevance-scored keep set for semantic compaction.
  604      ///
  605      /// Embeds the last `goal_window_turns` messages to represent the current
  606      /// goal, then scores all active messages by cosine similarity. Returns the
  607      /// cutoff index: messages before the cutoff will be summarized, messages at
  608      /// or after are kept verbatim.
  609      ///
  610      /// Messages above `relevance_keep_threshold` anywhere in the history are
  611      /// pulled out of the summarize set. Falls back to the standard recency
  612      /// cutoff if embeddings fail.
  613      fn semantic_cutoff(&mut self, active: &[Message]) -> usize {
  614          let goal_window_turns = self.compaction_config.goal_window_turns;

07
L1factmodelContext

07 · 上下文溢出与请求体过大走不同紧急恢复路径

SOURCE FACT

context-limit error 触发同步 hard compact 并重置 cache/tool/provider session；HTTP 413 类 byte-size 错误先从持久历史剥离超大 inline images；超长 OpenAI encrypted compaction 则丢 native state 改文本 fallback。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

“字太多”“图片字节太大”“服务端压缩包本身太大”不是同一种病，分别治疗。

IMPLICATION

避免对 413 做无效 token 摘要；紧急路径会改写持久 transcript，报告需明确这是有损恢复。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 90–135
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 136–182
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

Narrative from historical evidence ledger · 119301ac9d60 · open ledger commit ↗
crates/jcode-app-core/src/agent/compaction.rs:90–182PINNED ↗
   90      fn is_context_limit_error(error: &str) -> bool {
   91          let lower = error.to_lowercase();
   92          lower.contains("context length")
   93              || lower.contains("context window")
   94              || lower.contains("maximum context")
   95              || lower.contains("max context")
   96              || lower.contains("token limit")
   97              || lower.contains("too many tokens")
   98              || lower.contains("prompt is too long")
   99              || lower.contains("input is too long")
  100              || lower.contains("request too large")
  101              || lower.contains("length limit")
  102              || lower.contains("maximum tokens")
  103              || (lower.contains("exceeded") && lower.contains("tokens"))
  104      }
  105
  106      /// Best-effort emergency recovery after a context-limit error.
  107      ///
  108      /// Performs a synchronous hard compaction and resets provider session state,
  109      /// allowing the caller to retry the same turn immediately.
  110      pub(super) fn try_auto_compact_after_context_limit(&mut self, error: &str) -> bool {
  111          if crate::provider::openai_request::is_openai_encrypted_content_too_large_error(error)
  112              && self.try_recover_oversized_openai_native_compaction()
  113          {
  114              return true;
  115          }
  116          // A provider HTTP 413 ("request too large") is a *byte-size* failure
  117          // driven by inline base64 images, not a token-context overflow. Token
  118          // accounting deliberately undercounts images, so ordinary compaction
  119          // would not shrink the payload and the retry would 413 again. Strip
  120          // oversized images first.
  121          if self.try_recover_after_payload_too_large(error) {
  122              return true;
  123          }
  124          if !Self::is_context_limit_error(error) {
  125              return false;
  126          }
  127          if !self.provider.supports_compaction() {
  128              return false;
  129          }
  130
  131          let context_limit = self.provider.context_window() as u64;
  132          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  133
  134          let (dropped, usage_pct) = match compaction.try_write() {
  135              Ok(mut manager) => {
  136                  let (dropped, usage_pct) = {
  137                      let all_messages = self.session.provider_messages();
  138                      manager.update_observed_input_tokens(context_limit);
  139                      let usage_pct = manager.context_usage_with(all_messages) * 100.0;
  140                      let dropped = match manager.hard_compact_with(all_messages) {
  141                          Ok(dropped) => dropped,
  142                          Err(reason) => {
  143                              logging::warn(&format!(
  144                                  "Context-limit auto-recovery failed: hard compact failed ({})",
  145                                  reason
  146                              ));
  147                              return false;
  148                          }
  149                      };
  150                      (dropped, usage_pct)
  151                  };
  152                  self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  153                  (dropped, usage_pct)
  154              }
  155              Err(_) => {
  156                  logging::warn("Context-limit auto-recovery skipped: compaction manager lock busy");
  157                  return false;
  158              }
  159          };
  160
  161          self.cache_tracker.reset();
  162          self.locked_tools = None;
  163          self.provider_session_id = None;
  164          self.session.provider_session_id = None;
  165
  166          logging::warn(&format!(
  167              "Context limit exceeded; auto-compacted and retrying (dropped {} messages, usage was {:.1}%)",
  168              dropped, usage_pct
  169          ));
  170          crate::runtime_memory_log::emit_event(
  171              crate::runtime_memory_log::RuntimeMemoryLogEvent::new(
  172                  "auto_compaction_applied",
  173                  "context_limit_auto_compaction",
  174              )
  175              .with_session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  176              .with_detail(format!(
  177                  "dropped_messages={dropped},usage_pct={usage_pct:.1}"
  178              ))
  179              .force_attribution(),
  180          );
  181
  182          true
  183      }
  184
  185      /// Best-effort recovery after a provider HTTP 413 "request too large" error.
  186      ///
  187      /// This failure is caused by the serialized request body (dominated by inline
  188      /// base64 images) exceeding the provider's size cap, which is independent of
  189      /// the token context window. We strip oversized images from the persisted
  190      /// transcript, oldest-first, down to a conservative byte budget and reset the
  191      /// provider session/cache so the caller can retry the same turn immediately.
  192      fn try_recover_after_payload_too_large(&mut self, error: &str) -> bool {
  193          if !crate::compaction::is_request_payload_too_large_error(error) {
  194              return false;
  195          }
  196
  197          let stripped = self
  198              .session
  199              .strip_oversized_images(crate::compaction::PAYLOAD_IMAGE_CHAR_BUDGET);
  200          if stripped == 0 {
  201              logging::warn(
  202                  "Request-too-large recovery skipped: no oversized inline images to strip",
  203              );
  204              return false;
  205          }
  206
  207          // The transcript changed; reseed compaction bookkeeping and reset
  208          // provider session/cache state so the retry sends the reduced payload.
  209          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  210          if let Ok(mut manager) = compaction.try_write() {
  211              let provider_messages = self.session.messages_for_provider();
  212              manager.reset();
  213              manager.set_budget(self.provider.context_window());
  214              if let Some(state) = self.session.compaction.as_ref() {
  215                  manager.restore_persisted_state_with(state, &provider_messages);
  216              } else {
  217                  manager.seed_restored_messages_with(&provider_messages);
  218              }
  219              self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  220          }
  221
  222          self.cache_tracker.reset();
  223          self.locked_tools = None;
  224          self.provider_session_id = None;
  225          self.session.provider_session_id = None;
  226
  227          logging::warn(&format!(
  228              "Request body exceeded provider size limit; stripped {} oversized inline image(s) and retrying",
  229              stripped
  230          ));
  231          crate::runtime_memory_log::emit_event(
  232              crate::runtime_memory_log::RuntimeMemoryLogEvent::new(
  233                  "payload_too_large_recovered",
  234                  "request_payload_too_large",
  235              )
  236              .with_session_id(self.session.id.clone())
  237              .with_detail(format!("images_stripped={stripped}"))
  238              .force_attribution(),
  239          );
  240
  241          true
  242      }
  243
  244      fn try_recover_oversized_openai_native_compaction(&mut self) -> bool {
  245          let compaction = self.registry.compaction();
  246          let recovered = match compaction.try_write() {
  247              Ok(mut manager) => {
  248                  if !manager.discard_oversized_openai_native_compaction() {
  249                      return false;
  250                  }
  251                  self.sync_session_compaction_state_from_manager(&manager);
  252                  true
  253              }

08
L1factmodelContext

08 · 跨会话 memory 是独立后台 Agent，不阻塞主 turn

SOURCE FACT

MemoryAgent 通过容量 16 的 channel 接收主 Agent context，使用 embedding 检索、Haiku/sidecar relevance judge 和 memory-only tools；结果放入 pending memory，下一次新 user turn 才消费。

PLAIN LANGUAGE

主 Agent 回答时，旁边有个只管记忆的小秘书；它来不及的结果不会卡住当前回答，而是下一轮再递纸条。

IMPLICATION

延迟低且职责隔离，但 memory freshness 天生滞后一轮，channel 满时 try_send 可能丢更新。

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 1–22
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 23–45
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.
